Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the giants object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 221. The moduli space has dimension at most 330 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to giants structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 221/330 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in giants theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 905 and the invariant I(A,B) = 621 mod 1.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on giants structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 677. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 22 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 677 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the giants structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 22. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
